- **gestor-de-reclamos-y-devoluciones**: Clasificación y gestión de reclamos, devoluciones y disputas con carriers en pymes. Invocar cuando: un cliente reclame demoras o roturas, haya que gestionar un arrepentimiento de compra o armar un caso de siniestro con el carrier. ([raimondifernando-web/claude-catalogo](https://github.com/raimondifernando-web/claude-catalogo/blob/HEAD/plugins/rubro-finanzas-legal/agents/gestor-de-reclamos-y-devoluciones.md))
- **gestor-documentacion**: Especialista en documentación de proyectos Forge. Mantiene sincronizados README, CLAUDE.md del proyecto y docs técnicos. IMPORTANTE: NO tocar los archivos de infraestructura Forge (.claude/). Solo documentar el código del proyecto (src/, docs/, README.md). Indicarle qué archivos fueron modificados. ([Carlos-Dominguez-faber/inventario-pro](https://github.com/Carlos-Dominguez-faber/inventario-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestor-documentacion.md) · ★7 · 6 copies)
- **gestor-documentacion**: Especialista experto en documentación. Actualiza proactivamente la documentación cuando se realizan cambios en el código, garantiza la precisión del README y mantiene documentación técnica integral. Asegúrate de proporcionar a este subagente información sobre los archivos que fueron modificados para que sepa dónde buscar para documentar cambios. Siempre llama a este agente después de cambios en el código. ([desibarra/ebook-creator](https://github.com/desibarra/ebook-creator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestor-documentacion.md) · 9 copies)
- **gestor-documentacion**: Especialista en documentación. Invócalo SOLO tras cambios ESTRUCTURALES: feature nueva, cambio de API o contratos, dependencia añadida/eliminada, cambio de configuración o breaking change — no tras cada edición menor. Pásale la lista de archivos modificados y un resumen de qué cambió. NO usar para validar código (usa validacion-calidad) ni para explorar el repo (usa explorador-rapido). ([guaderrama/calude-code-base](https://github.com/guaderrama/calude-code-base/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestor-documentacion.md))
- **gestor-fluxo-chamados**: Use proativamente quando um chamado precisar avançar de estado (aberto → em andamento → resolvido), quando houver risco de violação de SLA, ou quando for preciso priorizar e rotear chamados entre as filas de produto. ([brendonbachie/gerenciador-de-chamados-curso-pratico](https://github.com/brendonbachie/gerenciador-de-chamados-curso-pratico/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestor-fluxo-chamados.md))
- **gestor-forja**: Usar con /faro:forja para operar la forja (GitHub/GitLab) — PRs a main con auto-merge, protección de ramas, pipeline, releases, topics, miembros y archivar. A main solo por PR con CI verde; nunca borra repos. ([nuvaweb/faro-harness](https://github.com/nuvaweb/faro-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/faro/agents/gestor-forja.md))
- **gestor-lista-de-espera**: Úsalo cuando una clínica o consulta quiera un sistema de lista de espera que funcione para volver a llenar rápido las cancelaciones de última hora: cómo registrar, priorizar, avisar y cerrar el hueco sin caos ni dobles reservas. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/salud-alternativas-2/agents/gestor-lista-de-espera.md))
- **gestor-pedidos**: Especialista em gestão de pedidos na Tray. Utilize para operações complexas envolvendo o ciclo completo do pedido: criação, atualização de status, emissão de notas fiscais, geração de etiquetas de envio e fluxo de cancelamento. ([tray-tecnologia/tray-api-ai-plugin](https://github.com/tray-tecnologia/tray-api-ai-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/gestor-pedidos.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **gestor-proyectos**: Gestiona los proyectos del vault: resume su estado, prepara la revisión semanal y mantiene al día las notas de `01 Proyectos/`. Úsalo para «estado de mis proyectos», «prepara la revisión» o «ficha de un proyecto». No toca otros repos. ([jmstudio14/cerebrojm](https://github.com/jmstudio14/cerebrojm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestor-proyectos.md))
- **gestor-pruebas**: Organiza las pruebas en Xray o k0lmenaTMT — carpetas, casos, ciclos y vínculos — con confirmación. Úsalo para subir o sincronizar casos o crear ciclos. ([underc0delabs/k0lmenIA](https://github.com/underc0delabs/k0lmenIA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestor-pruebas.md) · ★11)
- **gestor-requisitos**: Usar para el registro de requisitos (faro/gestion/requisitos.md) y la digestión de faro/fuentes/ — extrae requisitos de transcripciones, briefs o funcionales y atiende los avisos [req]/[digestion]. ([nuvaweb/faro-harness](https://github.com/nuvaweb/faro-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/faro/agents/gestor-requisitos.md))
- **gestor-resenas-y-valoraciones-app**: Convierte las valoraciones de las tiendas de apps en un sistema que funcione: cuándo y cómo activar el aviso de valoración dentro de la app, cómo responder a las reseñas en cada tienda, cómo extraer de ellas errores reales y demanda de funciones, y cómo recuperar una media dañada. Úsalo cuando la valoración de la app sea baja, cuando lleguen reseñas de una estrella, cuando el usuario quiera más valoraciones (pedidas a todos por igual, sin filtrar por satisfacción) o cuando las reseñas deban convertirse en una lista de tareas de producto. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/movil-varios/agents/gestor-resenas-y-valoraciones-app.md))
- **gestor-riesgos**: Usar para gestionar el RAID (faro/gestion/risks.md) — registrar riesgos, asunciones, impedimentos y dependencias, corregir estados, revisar los parados y atender los avisos [riesgos]/[ritmo]/[ria] (RIA e ISO/IEC 42001). ([nuvaweb/faro-harness](https://github.com/nuvaweb/faro-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/faro/agents/gestor-riesgos.md))
- **gestor-servicio-cv**: Coordina de principio a fin un encargo de pago de CV y carta de presentación para un cliente: reúne los datos del encargo, reparte el trabajo entre auditoría ATS y redacción, arma el paquete final y prepara el aviso de entrega. Se activa lanzándolo con Task cuando hay un encargo de cliente completo que gestionar, no solo revisar un CV suelto. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/empleo-varios/agents/gestor-servicio-cv.md))
- **gestor-servicio-registro-marca**: Coordina de principio a fin un encargo de cliente para comprobar y, si procede, iniciar el registro de una marca en España, desde la propuesta comercial hasta el seguimiento del expediente. Se activa cuando el usuario recibe el encargo de un cliente para este servicio. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/legal/agents/gestor-servicio-registro-marca.md))
- **gestor-tokens**: Especialista em Claude Code e consumo de tokens. Use para diagnosticar gasto alto, decidir modelo e esforço, organizar sessões e projetos, preparar compactação sem perder trabalho, e ensinar o jeito certo de usar a ferramenta. Use proativamente quando o contexto passar de 60%, ao começar um projeto novo, ou quando o usuário parecer perdido entre sessões. ([dougtudino/claude-gestor-tokens](https://github.com/dougtudino/claude-gestor-tokens/blob/HEAD/agents/gestor-tokens.md))
- **gestore**: Gestore di finanza e qualità del caso. Argomenta il preventivo all'avvio, a fine giro e quando il conto avvisa confronta costo e guadagno misurato e decide se proseguire, tagliare, cambiare modello a un ruolo che non misura o fermarsi. Non entra nel merito del documento. ([DesignRights-it/marketplace](https://github.com/DesignRights-it/marketplace/blob/HEAD/core/agents/gestore.md) · ★1)
- **gestore-ritorni**: Gestore dei ritorni e dei bug. Usalo quando Andrea (o utenti/clienti) segnala bug, malfunzionamenti, feedback o richieste di modifica su sviluppi già fatti; li registra, li classifica, li riproduce e ne segue lo stato fino alla chiusura. ([newalphalab/avanza](https://github.com/newalphalab/avanza/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestore-ritorni.md))

- **gesture-engineer**: Adds one gesture to the show language through the CLAUDE.md "Add a gesture" checklist, test first, and runs the suite. Never touches a show. Dispatched only by the director. - **gesture-engineer**: Gesture interaction specialist. Use when implementing drag, swipe, pinch, long-press, or other gesture-driven animations. - **get-current-datetime**: Return the current date and time in America/New_York. **Use this agent — do not invoke `date` directly via Bash** — whenever you need a real timestamp — converting "today"/"tomorrow"/"in N minutes/hours/days" to an absolute date, naming a file or backup folder with a timestamp, prefixing a log or decision entry, or stamping a memory record. Supports default human-readable, ISO 8601, filename-safe, date-only, and UTC variant formats. ([tanush753/claude-code-stack](https://github.com/tanush753/claude-code-stack/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/get-current-datetime.md))
- **get-default-access-controls-least-privilege**: `IAgentFactory.GetDefaultAccessControls` fills a temporary `Access Control Buffer` used when an instance is created. Permission sets that exist only as user-created sets in a sandbox are missing in the next environment. Granting `D365 BUS FULL ACCESS` or SUPER gives the agent a user-sized blast radius. Effective rights are still the intersection with the assigning user's permissions. ([microsoft/BCQuality](https://github.com/microsoft/BCQuality/blob/HEAD/community/knowledge/agents/get-default-access-controls-least-privilege.md) · ★247 · 7 copies)

- **getInstalledAgentsAction**: Retrieves action configurations for all installed agents. ([codeboltai/codeboltdocs](https://github.com/codeboltai/codeboltdocs/blob/HEAD/docs/05_reference/04_client-sdk/02_api-reference/agents/getInstalledAgentsAction.md) · ★2)
- **getirbakim-developer**: Implements a plan or issue inside a getirbakim git worktree — code, idempotent db/*.sql changes, tests, lint/typecheck, conventional commits. Give it the worktree path, the issue number and the plan. It never commits on dev/main, never pushes to main, never merges, never deploys. ([aokcuoglu/getirbakim](https://github.com/aokcuoglu/getirbakim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/getirbakim-developer.md))
- **getirbakim-planner**: Read-only architect for getirbakim. Give it a GitHub issue number (or a feature description) and the worktree path; it surveys the code and returns an executable implementation plan — files, reusable components/helpers, SQL/data impact, auth checks, tests to add or run, QA routes. Use before getirbakim-developer on anything bigger than a one-file fix. ([aokcuoglu/getirbakim](https://github.com/aokcuoglu/getirbakim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/getirbakim-planner.md))
- **getirbakim-qa**: QA runner for a getirbakim worktree. Runs the deterministic checks (admin-ui-lint, typecheck, lint, the relevant node tests) unless the brief says the developer already ran them on the current HEAD SHA, then — when routes are given — serves the worktree on a short-lived port, walks the routes in Chrome on desktop and mobile widths as the right role, checks console/network errors, and returns a pass/fail report with repro steps. Give it the worktree path, routes/roles and the acceptance criteria. It verifies; it does not fix. ([aokcuoglu/getirbakim](https://github.com/aokcuoglu/getirbakim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/getirbakim-qa.md))
- **getirbakim-reviewer**: Read-only code reviewer for getirbakim branches. Give it a worktree path (or PR number); it reviews the diff against origin/dev for authorization, SQL safety and data integrity, Next.js 16 server/client boundaries, supplier-sync and commerce correctness, performance on ~1M-row tables, tests and flow hygiene, and returns a structured verdict. Use after getirbakim-developer and before opening the PR. ([aokcuoglu/getirbakim](https://github.com/aokcuoglu/getirbakim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/getirbakim-reviewer.md))
- **getirbakim-triage**: Backlog triage for getirbakim GitHub issues. Reads every open issue, checks merged PRs and the code on origin/dev to classify each as done, partly done, duplicate, obsolete or open (with evidence), changes nothing itself, and returns proposed actions, a priority order and the next conflict-free parallel wave. Use via /backlog or when the user asks what is left. ([aokcuoglu/getirbakim](https://github.com/aokcuoglu/getirbakim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/getirbakim-triage.md))
- **getirbakim-ui-ux**: Design-system and UX reviewer for getirbakim UI changes. Give it a worktree path (and optionally routes + a running server URL); it audits .tsx/.css changes against the /yonetim stock-shadcn rule in AGENTS.md and the storefront language in DESIGN.md — tokens, status variants, tables, states, mobile, accessibility, Turkish copy — and can screenshot routes with the Chrome tools. Returns findings; never edits code. ([aokcuoglu/getirbakim](https://github.com/aokcuoglu/getirbakim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/getirbakim-ui-ux.md))

- **gewerberecht-drafter**: Du bist die **Entwurfsstufe**. - **gfd-architect**: grace-feature-dev architect. Designs ONE decisive feature blueprint for a given focus (minimal-change / clean / pragmatic) by analyzing existing patterns, and emits it as TWO orthogonal projections — a DraftCodeGraph (modules/files/calls) and a step-by-step Data Flow — plus acceptance criteria and a vertical-slice list. Read-only. Spawned 2-3 in parallel; the orchestrator collapses the alternatives. ([maksimovyar/grace-board](https://github.com/maksimovyar/grace-board/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gfd-architect.md))
- **gfd-coder**: grace-feature-dev implementer for fanout/hybrid mode. Implements ONE board card (a feature slice) in fresh context — code + tests — applying GRACE markup per the run's rigor, LDD logs, and BUG_FIX_CONTEXT scars. Touches only the card's files[]. Returns a structured result. Used only when the orchestrator scales out; in inline mode the main thread codes instead. ([maksimovyar/grace-board](https://github.com/maksimovyar/grace-board/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gfd-coder.md))
- **gfd-coder-frontend**: grace-feature-dev implementer for SCREEN cards (card type `screen`). Same contract as gfd-coder — one card, fresh context, code + tests, touches only the card's files[] — but built for UI: it loads Skill(frontend-design) before writing markup/styles and answers to the project's own design tokens instead of inventing a look per screen. Spawned by the orchestrator in hybrid/fanout mode when the board's type table routes a screen card here. ([maksimovyar/grace-board](https://github.com/maksimovyar/grace-board/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gfd-coder-frontend.md))
- **gfd-explorer**: grace-feature-dev codebase explorer. Read-only. Traces execution paths, maps abstraction layers, and documents patterns/dependencies to inform new development. Navigates cheaply — grep by GREP_SUMMARY/STRUCTURE markers first, semantic reading only when needed. Spawned 2-3 in parallel during Exploration, each on a different aspect. Returns 5-10 essential files to read. Never edits code. ([maksimovyar/grace-board](https://github.com/maksimovyar/grace-board/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gfd-explorer.md))
- **gfd-reviewer**: grace-feature-dev static code reviewer. Reviews ONE card's diff FILE (produced by the orchestrator) hunk by hunk against a fixed checklist for its focus (bugs/correctness OR simplicity/DRY), and must return a coverage table for every hunk. Only blocking findings (confidence ≥ 80, blocking class) send the card back; style goes to notes. Read-only. Spawned 1-2 in parallel per card in the Review phase, after tests are green and the markup linter is clean. ([maksimovyar/grace-board](https://github.com/maksimovyar/grace-board/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gfd-reviewer.md))
- **gfd-verifier**: grace-feature-dev independent verifier. Read-only. Runs the card's tests against the real code and performs Semantic Trace Verification — does the actual execution path in the logs match the DevelopmentPlan Data Flow? — then returns a structured Bug Report. When rigor=off it verifies behavior/acceptance-criteria instead of LDD markers. Spawned once per card in the Verify phase. Does NOT fix code. ([maksimovyar/grace-board](https://github.com/maksimovyar/grace-board/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gfd-verifier.md))
